EXCEPT THE CONTENTION THAT "VETERAN U.N. DIPLOMATS WHO WORKED. . . 
SINCE THE FOUNDATION OF THIS ORGANIZATION CANNOT REMEMBER A SPEECH BY 
ANY OTHER STATESMAN THAT MADE SUCH AN EXTRAORDINARY IMPRESSION AS THAT 
OF KHRUSHCHEV,” 

BOTH PRAVDA AND THE GOVERNMENT ORGAN IZVESTIA RAN THE FULL TEXT OF 
KHRUSHCHEV'S SPEECH BEFORE THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY. 

PRAVDA TOPPED ITS FRONT PAGE WITH A SIX-COLUMN HEADLINE SAYING 
*SOVIET GOVERNMENT PROPOSES GENERAL AND TOTAL DISARMAMENT OF ALL 

ANOTHER FRONT PAGE HEADLINE SAID THE SOVIET PROPOSALS WERE 
“PROMPTED EXCLUSIVELY BY DESIRE TO SECURE A REALLY LASTING PEACE AMONG

CLEAR CONFERENCE DECLARED OVER MOSCOW RADIO'S HOME SERVICE THAT KHRUSH- 
CHEV HAD "SHOWED HOW TO SOLVE THE PROBLEM OF DISARMAMENT MOST SIMPLY®.

PREMIER NIkiTA e.* 19 (AP)-THE "TOTAL DISARMAMENT" SPEECH BY SOVIET 


AS ORIGINALLY PRESENTED, THE PLAN CALLED FOR DISBANDING OF ALL 
ARMED FORCES, ENDING ALL MILITARY TRAINING. SCRAPPING ALL WARSHIPS 
HALTING ALL MANUFACTURE OF ARMAMENTS AND FORBIDDING THE ISSUANCE OF 
PATENTS ON MILITARY DEVICES. ! 

THE LEAGUE'S PREPARATORY DISARMAMENT COMMISSION RULED IN 1929 
THAT THE PLAN WAS “OUTSIDE ITS SCOPE," £43 2 

DISTRUST OF THE PLAN AT THE 2933=DISARMAMENT CONFERENCE WAS 
REFLECTED BY SALVADOR DE MADARIAGA, THE SPANISH DELEGATE. 

HE TOLD AN ANECDOTE OF AN ANIMAL DISARMAMENT CONFERENCE. 

IN IT. THE LION LOOKED AT THE EAGLE AND SAID TALONS MUST BE 
ABOLISHED. THE TIGER LOOKED AT THE ELEPHANT AND SAID TUSKS MUST BE 
ABOLISHED, THE ELEPHANT LOOKED AT THE TIGER AND SAID JAWS AND CLAWS 

"THUS EACH ANIMAL IN TURN," SAID DE MADARIAGA, "PROPOSED THE 
ABOLITION OF WEAPONS HE DID NOT HAVE, UNTIL AT LAST THE BEAR ROSE UP 
AND SAID IN TONE'S OF SWEET REASONABLENESS: 


"*COMRADES, LET US ABOLISH EVERYTHING--EVERYTHING BUT THE GREAT 
UNIVERSAL EMBRACE.**

° 
PUSHING THIS PROPOSAL, KHRUSHCHEV TOLD HIS LOS ANGELES AUDIENCES 
"WE WANT TO SOLVE THE PROBLEM OF DISARMAMENT IN ITS ENTIRETY, ONCE 
AND FOREVER: TO PUT STATES IN SUCH CONDITIONS WHEN THEY WOULD BE 
DEPRIVED OF THE MATERIAL MEANS OF WARFARE. IS IT NOT A FACT THAT ONE 
CAN HARDLY SUPPOSE THAT LACKING PLANES, TANKS, MISSILES AND OTHER 
MODERN WEAPONS, AMERICANS AND RUSSIANS WOULD START FIGHTING, HAVING 
ARMED THEMSELVES WITH FORKS AND TABLE KNIVES, FOR INSTANCE? 
KHRUSHCHEV'S TOTAL DISARMAMENT PROPOSAL ALREADY HAS BEEN MET WITH 
CONSIDERABLE RESERVE, NOT ONLY BY THE WEST BUT BY OTHER NATIONS 
REPRESENTED IN THE U.N., WHOSE REPRESENTATIVES APPARENTLY REGARDED IT

saad WANTED TO KNOW WHETHER THE RUSSIANS “TAKE US FOR MORONS.®

"WE REALIZE NATURALLY,” KHRUSHCHEV TOLD THE CIVIC BANQUET, *THAT 
THE IDEA OF DISARMAMENT HAS QUITE A FEW ENEMIES. BUT WE SHOULD LIKE 
TO BELIEVE THAT OUR NEW PROPOSALS MADE IN PERFECT GOOD FAITH AND 
REPRESENTING THE GREATEST LENGTH TO WHICH A GREAT POWER CAN GO 
ESPECIALLY IN THE CONDITIONS OF THE PRESENT-DAY INTERNATIONAL TENSIONS, 
WILL FIND A POSITIVE RESPONSE IN THE U.S.A."

ORDERLY CROWDS. HIGHLY CURIOUS BUT NOT OVER-EXCITED, WITNESSED THE 
SOVIET PREMIER'S ARRIVAL AT THE 20TH CENTURY FOX STUDIOS WHERE THE 
LUNCHEON WAS HELD IN HIS HONOR. 

HIS EYES SPARKLING. KHRUSHCHEV REPEATEDLY SHOUTED "OKAY, OKAY" TO 
THE THRONG OF CELEBRITIES SURROUNDING HIM, TAKING FULLEST ADVANTAGE OF 
ONE OF THE FEW WORDS OF ENGLISH HE APPARENTLY MASTERED IN HIS U.S. TOUR.

KHRUSHCHEV WAS WHISKED FROM NEW YORK IN A HUGE MILITARY AIR TRANS-

WITTOLLYWOOD. SEPT. 19 (AP)-MRS.NIKITA KHRUSHCHEV TALKED ANIMAT- 
THROUGH’ A MOVIE LUNCHEON TODAY AND CHARMED TABLE PARTNERS BOB HOPE, 
FRANK SINATRA AND DAVID NIVEN. 1 

*I THOUGHT SHE WAS VERY CHARMING, VERY SINCERE AND VERY SWEET," HOPE 
SAID AFTERWARD. SHE SEEMED VERY INTERESTED IN ALL OF THE PEOPLE’ AT THE 
LUNCHEON. EVEN THOUGH SHE DIDN'T KNOW WHO WE WERE. BUT HER INTERPRETER 
INTRODUCED US." 

OTHER STARS, SUCH AS DINAH SHORE, ESTHER WILLIAMS AND JOAN COLLINS, 
COMMENTED ON HER WARMTH AND HER INTERESTING FACE. 

TO SINATRA THE PLUMP LITTLE WIFE OF THE SOVIET BOSS WAS "A DARLING, 
CHARMING WOMAN OF GREAT WISDOM. NIVEN CALLED HER "ABSOLUTELY LOVELY. 

MRS.K SPEAKS AND UNDERSTANDS ENOUGH ENGLISH FOR TABLE CONVERSATION, 
BUT WAS AIDED OCCASIONALLY BY THE INTERPRETER. 

HOPE GAVE THE FOLLOWING ACCOUNT OF THE LUNCHEON: 

"THE INTERPRETER, WHO HAS LIVED IN NEW YORK AND SO KNEW US, WAS 
ESPECIALLY FOND OF GARY COOPER. HE WAS BROUGHT OVER FOR AN INTRODUCTIOW 
"YOU KNOW WHAT A GREAT AD LIBBER COOPER IS. HE SAID TO HER, ‘WHY 
DON’T YOU MOVE OUT HERE? YOU'LL LIKE THE CLIMATE.’ SHE REPLIED 

QUICKLY, "NO, MOSCOW IS ALL RIGHT FOR ME.’ 

“THEY SEEMED TO BE VERY DEFENSIVE ABOUT THIS SORT OF THING. 

“SHE TOLD ME ABOUT WHAT SHE HAD SEEN AND LIKED IN THIS COUNTRY AND I 
TOLD HER ABOUT MY MOSCOW TRIP. I WAS SURPRISED THAT SHE ASKED QUESTIONS 
IN ENGLISH. SHE SEEMED TO UNDERSTAND MOST OF THE TIME. 

"SHE WAS THE ONE WHO WAS SO INTENT ON GOING TO DISNEYLAND. ‘I WANT 
TO GO, SHE SAID. THEY TOLD HER SHE COULDN'T BECAUSE OF 
SECURITY REASONS. SHE SENT A NOTE UP TO HER HUSBAND AND THAT'S WHAT 
TOUCHED OFF THE WHOLE THING (KHRUSHCHEV'’S PROTEST DURING HIS ADDRESS 
AT NOT GOING TO DISNEYLAND). 

HE (KHRUSHCHEV) JUMPED UP AND DOWN ABOUT IT UNTIL THE HULA HOOPS 
FELL OFF AND REALLY CARRIED ON. I GUESS HE WANTED TO SEE A ROCKET THAT 


WORKS.

KHRUSHCHEV MOVIE STUDIO (700) 
BY RELMAN MORIN 

HOLLYWOOD. SEPT. 19 (AP)-NIKITA S.KHRUSHCHEV CAME TO HOLLYwooD TODAY 
AND OUT-STARRED THE STARS WITH THE MOST SPECTACULAR ACT OF HIS AMERICAN 


SOME WERE STUNNED, SOME WERE ANGRY AND ALL WERE STARTLED. 

KHRUSHCHEV INTERRUPTED SPEAKERS. HE ALLOWED HIMSELF TOO BE INTER- 
RUPTED, HE SPOKE WITH SUCH VEHEMENCE THAT HE APOLOGIZED BEFORE AND 
AFTER HIS REMARKS. 

THE SOVIET PREMIER STOOD FOR MORE THAN 45 MINUTES IN THE MAIN DINING 
ROOM AT 2OTHE CENTURY FOX STUDIOS, ADDRESSING AN AUDIE 
ROOM AT 20TH CENTURY FOX STUDIOS, ADDRESSING AN AUDIENCE FILLED WITH 
MANY OF THE BEST KNOWN ACTORS, ACTRESSES , DIRECTORS, WRITERS AND MOTION 
PICTURE EXECUTIVES. 

AT A TABLE DIRECTLY IN FRONT OF HIM, HIS WIFE SAT BETWEEN BOB HOPE 
AND FRANK SINATRA. ONE OF HIS DAUGHTERS HAD DAVID NIVEN ON HER RIGHT. 

FROM ACROSS THE ROOM MARILYN MONROE, WEARING A LOW CUT BLACK DRESS, 
LEFT OFF READING A MOVIE SCRIPT TO STAND AND STARE SEVERAL TIMES AT 

* 

GARY COOPER CAME OVER TO MRS.KHRUSHCHEV TO BE INTRODUCED. 

AT ANOTHER TABLE ELIZABETH TAYLOR SAT LOOKING DREAMILY TOWARD THE 
CEILING, HER HUSBAND, EDDIE FISHER, SAT BESIDE HER. ARLENE DAHL. KIM 
NOVAK, SHIRLEY MACLAINE, JUDY GARLAND AND SCORES OF OTHER LUMINARTES-- 
WHO HAD WON OUT IN THE FURIOUS STRUGGLE FOR INVITATIONS TO THE 
LUNCHEON--BENT FORWARD, AS THOUGH HYPNOTIZED BY THE SOVIET LEADER. 

IT WAS AN ALMOST INCREDIBLE SPECTACLE, 

HERE WAS THE ESSENCE OF REALITY IN THE FORM OF THE POWERFUL SOVIET 
DICTATOR, CONFRONTING THE ELITE OF THE LAND OF MAKE BELIEVE. 

KHRUSHCHEV, ALWAYS SENSITIVE TO THE MOOD OF HIS AUDIENCE, CAUGHT 
THE DRAMA AND GAVE THE MOVIE PEOPLE AN UNBELIEVABLE SHOW, 

AT TIMES HE CONVULSED THEM WITH LAUGHTER» AT OTHERS HE HAMMERED 
THE AIR WITH HIS BIG FIST AND HIS VOICE THUNDERED OVER THE MICRO- 
PHONES. HE PULLED OUT ALL THE STOPS IN A DISPLAY.OF THEATRICS THAT 
SURPASSED ANY OF HIS PREVIOUS APPEARANCES. 

HE BROKE IN OW SPYROS SKOURAS, HEAD OF 20TH CENTURY-FOX, HIS HOST, 
ON THREE OCCASIONS, WHEN UN AMBASSADOR HENRY CABOT LODGE RAISED ep 
SOVIET ARTISTS AND WRITERS, AND MENTIONED MIKHAIL SHOLOKOV —— A WR 
WHO WAS RECENTLY IN TROUBLE IN RUSSIA.-- KHRUSHCHEV SPOKE OUT; _HE 
SAID SHOLOKOV HAD RECENTLY WRITTEN “THE VIRGIN SOIL UPTURNEDs” CB 
WORK THAT REPORTEDLY DISPLEASED HIM AND SAID: “IT’S A G 


BUY IT." 


PE eeyeeces eons PS PRR a 


THE NO. 1 MAN OF RUSSIA STOOD GRINNING WHEN SKOURAS, IN TURN 
INTERRUPTED HIM. AND HE DEMONSTRATED AGAIN HIS RAPIER ABILITY TO 
GIVE AND TAKE IN UNSCHEDULED DEBATE. 

“HOW MANY PRIME MINISTERS ARE THERE IN RUSSIA?" SKOURAS ASKED. 

“HOW MANY PRESIDENTS OF THE UNITED STATES ARE THERE?” KHRUSHCHEV 

APPED. 
9 THERE WERE GASPS OF SURPRISE WHEN SKOURAS ROSE, PUT HIS ARM 
AROUND KHRUSHCHEV AND SAID: “THERE ARE TWO MILLION PRESIDENTS OF 
CORPORATIONS IN THE UNITED STATES. RUSSIA IS THE BIGGEST CAPITALIST 
MONOPOLY IN THE WORLD.® 


KHRUSHCHEV ALTERNATED SARDONIC, GLOVES-OFF CRITICISM OF THE UNiIzw 
STATES WITH FULSOME PRAISE. 

SPEAKING TO AN AUDIENCE OF FILM ARTISTSN HE ASSERTED THAT 
ARTISTIC ACHIEVEMENT COULD ONLY TAKE PLACE IN A FREE SOCIETY. HE SAID 
THE RUSSIAN VIEW OF FREEDOM IS DIFFERENT FROM THE AMERICAN VIEW. 

HE RECALLED THAT AMERICAN ENGINEERS HAD HELPED EQUIP RUSSIAN 
FACTORIES AND DESCRIBED THE RUSSIANS AS “THE PUPILS OF AMERICA.” 
THEN, SWIFTLY, HE BOASTED “WE ARE GOING TO SURPASS YOU." 

AND HE TWITTED HIS AUDIENCE WITH THE FACT THAT A SOVIET ROCKET 
STRUCK THE MOON AND THAT “OUR PENNANT IS THERE."

i KHRUSHCHEV ANALYSIS 

JOHN M, HIGHTOWER 

WASHINGTON, SEPT. 19 (AP)-NIKITA S. KHRUSHCHEV SEEMS TO BE 
DELIGHTED WITH THE RESULTS OF HIS FIRST FEW DAYS IN THE UNITED 
STATES. BUT U.S. OFFICIALS WHO HAVE HOPED FOR SOME SOFTENING OF 
SOVIET POLICY ARE DISAPPOINTED. 

THE SOVIET PREMIER WILL HOLD THREE DAYS OF POLICY TALKS WITH 
PRESIDENT EISENHOWER NEXT WEEK JUST BEFORE HE FLIES HOME. UNLESS HE 
PRODUCES SOME NEWER APPROACH TO WORLD PROBLEMS THAN HE HAS COME UP 


orm so sof) THE CONFERENCE AT CAMP DAVID, MD., PROMISES TO BE A 
REN AFFAIR. 

PROBABLY THE REASON FOR KHRUSHCHEV’S EVIDENT PLEASURE IN HIS VISITS 
TO WASHINGTON AND NEW YORK IS THAT HE IS HAVING A PROPAGANDA SUCCESS. 

IN HIS APPEARANCE AT THE NATIONAL PRESS CLUB WEDNESDAY, THE ONE- 
TIME COAL MINER PROVED HIMSELF A VOLUBLE SALESMAN OF COMMUNIST 
IDEOLOGICAL WARES AND AN ARTFUL DODGER OF QUESTIONS HE DISLIKED OR 
PREFERRED NOT TO MEET HEAD ON. 

BEFORE THE UNITED NATIONS GENERAL ASSEMBLY IN NEW YORK YESTERDAY, 
WHILE REPRESENTATIVES OF MORE THAN 80 OTHER NATIONS SAT MOSTLY IN 
EAGER SILENCE, HE OUTLINED HIS PLAN FOR SAVING THE WORLD FROM WAR. 

HE DID IT $0 SKILLFULLY AND WITH IMPLICATIONS OF SUCH TREMENDOUS 
HOPE BEHIND IT THAT U.S. OFFICIALS WERE STOPPED FROM SAYING WHAT THEY 
REALLY THOUGHT--THAT KHRUSHCHEV HAD HOAXED THE U.N., THAT HIS PLAN 
IS NOT A PLAN AT ALL BUT A GILDED PROPAGANDA STATEMENT OF A 
NOBLE, UTOPIAN, PRESENTLY UNATTAINABLE GOAL. 

“THE TERRIBLE THING ABOUT THIS,* ONE STATE DEPARTMENT OFFICIAL 
SAID PRIVATELY AFTER WATCHING KHRUSHCHEV'S UeN. SPEECH ON TELEVISION, 


“IS THAT YOU CAN'T BE AGAINST IT. EVERYBODY IS FOR TOTAL AND UNI- 


VERSAL DISARMAMENT. BUT THIS DOES NOT 
THE GOAL.® ADVANCE YOU ONE INCH TOWARD

aimee

WHAT KHRUSHCHEV DESCRIBED AS A PLAN-~COMPLETE DISARMAMENT IN FOUR 
YEARS BACKED UP BY A CONTROL SYSTEM ABOUT WHICH HE WAS VAGUE--WAS IN 
FACT ONE STATEMENT OF THE AIM OF 10-YEAR-OLD DISARMAMENT NEGOTIATIONS. 
THESE NEGOTIATIONS HAVE MADE ONE THING CLEAR ABOVE ALL OTHERS. THE 
CORE OF AN ARMS REDUCTION AND CONTROL AGREEMENT BETWEEN RUSSIA AND 

THE WESTERN POWERS IS THE KIND OF SYSTEM WHICH WOULD ENFORCE IT. 

BEHIND THIS CENTRAL ISSUE, IN TURN, LIES THE MISTRUST THAT 
RUSSIA AND THE WESTERN BLOC HAVE IN EACH OTHER'S PLEDGED WORD. BUT 
THE SOVIETS HAVE A TREMENDOUS ADVANTAGE HERE. UNDER THE WESTERN 
SYSTEM, WITH PUBLIC INQUIRY AND DEBATE AND WITH A FREE PRESS, THE 
SOVIETS CAN HAVE REASONABLE CONFIDENCE IN THEIR KNOWLEDGE OF THE 
READINESS FOR WAR OF WESTERN ARMAMENTS. 

IN THE SOVIET UNION THE REVERSE IS TRUE. RIGID CONTROLS OVER PRESS 
AND OVER LEGISLATIVE DISCUSSION AND OVER TRAVEL BY FOREIGNERS GIVE 
RUSSIA CONDITIONS OF SECRECY UNKNOWN IN THE WEST. 

ISARMAMENT EXPERTS BELIEVE IT WOULD BE RELATIVELY EASY FOR THE 
SOVIETS TO PREPARE A SNEAK ATTACK IF THE POLITICAL LEADERS THOUGHT 
THEY NEEDED WAR FOR THEIR PURPOSES. WER 

HENCE IT IS NOT THE LACK OF A DISARMAMENT AGREEMENT BUT THE LACF 
OF TRUST AND CONFIDENCE WHICH UNDERLIES THE TENSION BETWEEN RUSSIA 
AND THE WESTERN POWERS TODAY. THE REAL PROBLEM IS NOT THE EXISTENC- 
Z a Me MR ge ROCKETS BUT THE EXISTENCE OF SUSPICIC.

ENT HBetae TO THE EDITORS AND COMMENTS ON RADIO DISPLAY THIS

THE SCOPE OF THE SOVIET VIEW OF KHRUSHCHEV'S IMPACT ON ORDINAR 
AMERICANS WAS INDICATED BY A COMMENT IN IZVESTIA, THE GOVERNMENT

ee PRESS CLUB IN WASHINGTON WHEN THE SOVIET PREMIER SPOKE

EXPECTATIONS FROM THE VISIT. | . 

THE IMPRESSION GIVEN IS THAT THE TRIP COMES AS CONFIRMATION OF THE 
LONG-TIME SOVIET PROPAGANDA THEME THAT THE BASIC SYMPATHY OF ORDINARY 
AMERICANS IS FOR PERSONALITIES AND IDEAS OF THE SOVIETS. THIS THEME 
HOLDS THAT TENSION BETWEEN THE TWO COUNTRIES HAS BEEN FOMENTED BY A 
SMALL GROUP OF MILITARISTS AND IMPERIALISTS. 

RADIO PROGRAMS HAVE BEEN USING MANY AMERICAN MUSICAL COMPOSITIONS 
IN STRESSING THE BASIC SIMILARITIES OF THE TWO NATIONS. 

THE FORTHCOMING 150TH ANNIVERSARY OF DIPLOMATIC RELATIONS IS 


GETTING BIG PLAY. 


MOSCOW RADIO HAS BROADCAST A REPORT THAT HISTORIC RESEARCH

co el A dg 19 (AP)-THE CONVULSIVE SHAKEUP IN THE TOP 


ECHELONS OF THE RED CHINESE REGIME MAY TURN INTO A SWEEPING PURGE, 
STATE DEPARTMENT EXPERTS SAY. 

THE DEPARTMENT'S EXPERTS ON CHINA SAY THE VIOLENT RESHUFFLE ON THE 
EVE OF COMMUNIST CHINA'S 10TH ANNIVERSARY OF POWER CAN MEAN ONLY ONE 
THING: DEEP TROUBLE OVER THE COMMUNE SYSTEM SET UP A YEAR AGO, A 
SYSTEM THAT TRIED TO ORGANIZE THE CHINESE PEASANTRY INTO SEMI- 
MILITARY PRODUCTION UNITS. 

AT LEAST FOUR MEMBERS OF THE POWERFUL COMMUNIST CENTRAL COMMITTEE 
HAVE BEEN THROWN OUT WITH NO ANNOUNCEMENT THAT THEY WILL BE 
ASSIGNED TO OTHER JOBS. 

THESE ARE CHANG WEN-T'IEN AND WANG CHIA-HSIANG, VICE MINISTERS 
OF FOREIGN AFFAIRS, MARSHAL PENG TEH-HUAI, MINISTER OF DEFENSE, AND 
GEN. HUANG KO-CHENG, FORMER ARMY CHIEF OF STAFF. 

THESE NAMES MEAN NOTHING TO MOST AMERICANS. BUT PUT IT IN AMERI- 
CAN TERMS: SUPPOSE THE SECRETARY OF STATE'S TwO TOP DEPUTIES, THE 
SECRETARY OF DEFENSE AND A TOP ARMY ADVISOR ALL WERE SUDDENLY 
FIRED OUT OF HAND BY PRESIDENT EISENHOWER.

MN IN MAO TSE-TUNG'S CHINA.
